若 success：与 7B full_trace（~85.88% baseline report 趋势）和 1.5B 其他条件（exception_only 99.83%, VOTA 99.58%, no_trace 67.31%）对比，写入 ROADMAP 1.5B 表格\n  - 若 negative：按 red-section 产出 Framing 调整提案\n- qwen15b_full_trace 完成后 cuda:0 空闲 → 立即提交 **codellama13b_exception_only_seed42**（ROADMAP 标注最高优先级）\n- loop_only_seed456 cron 回调 → 分析 + DONE → cuda:1 空闲 → 提交 codellama13b_no_trace_seed42\n- 1.5B 四条件全部 DONE 后 → spawn Worker 做 prediction overlap 分析（1.5B vs 7B exception_only 错误样本对比，零 GPU cost）\n- CodeLlama 13B 全完成 → Paper Agent patch 整合新数据到 Table 1 + cross-scale analysis\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n- **核心结论**：exception events 是 verdict prediction 最有效的 trace component，跨 scale (1.5B/7B) 和跨家族验证。Qwen-7B exception_only 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds)，Qwen-1.5B exception_only 也是 99.83%（scale-invariant）\n- **当前阶段**：Round 5 审稿驱动的 scope expansion。1.5B grid 差 full_trace 一格就完成，CodeLlama-13B 0/3 尚未开始\n- **易错点**：full_trace 在 7B 上表现远不如 exception_only（DeepSeek full_trace v3 仅 79.16%），1.5B full_trace 预期也会低——这不是 negative，是印证 \"exception 才是 key signal\" 的正向证据。不要误判为实验失败\n- **9 天冲刺**：今天必须完成 1.5B grid + 启动 CodeLlama，明天 CodeLlama × 3 出结果，5/18 开始 paper patch。时间非常紧，cuda:0 空闲一小时都是浪费\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 79 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

### 3. 📊 当前状态
- **刚发生**：qwen15b_full_trace_seed42 cron 回调到达，状态 ANALYZING。我已 spawn worker (`worker_analysis_qwen15b_full_trace_seed42_20260516T120137`) 去 SSH 读 eval_results.json + 日志，**尚未收到回调**
- **RUNNING**：loop_only_seed456 (cuda:1, PID 269723)
- **ANALYZING**：qwen15b_full_trace_seed42 (cuda:0) — 等 Worker 回调后更新 metrics → DONE
- **QUEUED (13)**：codellama13b × 3 (exception_only/no_trace/vota)、qwen1.5b × 4、codellama_vota/no_trace/exception_only、exception_only_nomarker × 3 等
- **等待中**：Worker `worker_analysis_qwen15b_full_trace_seed42_20260516T120137` 的 DONE 回调

### 4. 📋 待办
- Worker 分析回调到达 → 读 metrics，判断 conclusion（success/negative），update_registry → DONE，更新 report.md
  - 若 success：与 7B full_trace（~85.88% baseline report 趋势）和 1.5B 其他条件（exception_only 99.83%, VOTA 99.58%, no_trace 67.31%）对比，写入 ROADMAP 1.5B 表格
  - 若 negative：按 red-section 产出 Framing 调整提案
- qwen15b_full_trace 完成后 cuda:0 空闲 → 立即提交 **codellama13b_exception_only_seed42**（ROADMAP 标注最高优先级）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心结论**：exception events 是 verdict prediction 最有效的 trace component，跨 scale (1.5B/7B) 和跨家族验证。Qwen-7B exception_only 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds)，Qwen-1.5B exception_only 也是 99.83%（scale-invariant）

- **易错点**：full_trace 在 7B 上表现远不如 exception_only（DeepSeek full_trace v3 仅 79.16%），1.5B full_trace 预期也会低——这不是 negative，是印证 "exception 才是 key signal" 的正向证据。不要误判为实验失败
- **9 天冲刺**：今天必须完成 1.5B grid + 启动 CodeLlama，明天 CodeLlama × 3 出结果，5/18 开始 paper patch。时间非常紧，cuda:0 空闲一小时都是浪费

Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

```text
Good. GPU whitelist confirmed: westd-11235:cuda:0 and cuda:1.

Now let me assess the current situation and report to Director. I need to:

1. Both GPUs should be in use:
   - `exception_only_nomarker_seed42` - RUNNING (since 06:03, that's been ~10 hours!)
   - `codellama13b_vota_seed42` - RUNNING (PID 279473, cron confirmed)

2. Key results to report:
   - 1.5B grid COMPLETE: full_trace 77.06%, exception_only 99.83%, VOTA 99.58%, no_trace 67.31%
   - CodeLlama-13B showing strong performance with exception_only at 99.83% (perfect cross-family match), no_trace at 76.5%, and VOTA still running
   - loop_only 3-seed averaging 86.05% ± 0.43 and complete, while exception_only_nomarker is actively removing format markers

3. Once codellama13b_vota finishes, the CodeLlama grid will be done, and then I can start the nomarker variants once exception_only_nomarker completes.

Now I'm waiting on the remaining seeds to finish, then I'll patch the paper with all the new results. I should focus on reporting to the Director since report.md auto-generates anyway. Let me check what reporting tools I have available and see how long exception_only_nomarker_seed42 has actually been running.

Looking at the registry, that experiment started at 06:03 UTC on 5/16 and was last updated around 15:51 UTC—nearly 10 hours for what should be a 1-2 hour run. The recent update timestamp suggests the cron is still monitoring it, so it hasn't crashed, but this duration is unusual and worth investigating. The cron reconciliation only showed one active experiment, which means exception_only_nomarker_seed42 either wasn't registered with a cron job or isn't actually running on a GPU despite appearing to be active. I need to verify whether it was started manually without proper scheduling or if something else is preventing it from being tracked.

Most of the pending work from the handoff has already been completed—the Qwen and CodeLlama results are already in the registry—so my focus now is reporting the current status to the Director and investigating the missing nomarker experiment.

I also need to respect the Worker pool constraint and keep at least 2 active, so I'll spawn one to investigate the nomarker status and another for the prediction overlap analysis that's been on the TODO list, then handle the next steps once CodeLlama finishes.
```

```json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：Prediction Overlap 分析 — Qwen-1.5B vs 7B exception_only 错误样本对比\n\n### 背景\nQwen-7B 和 Qwen-1.5B 在 exception_only 条件下都达到 99.83% accuracy (2/1190 errors)。我们想知道：两个 scale 犯的错是不是同一批样本？如果是，说明存在 \"universal irreducible ceiling\"（数据标注问题或 inherent ambiguity）。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：westd-11235，账号 root\n- Python：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 项目路径：/root/trace-verdict-study\n- Checkpoint 目录：/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/\n\n### 数据位置\n- 7B exception_only predictions: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/exception_only/final/predictions.jsonl`（seed42）\n- 1.5B exception_only predictions: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/qwen15b_exception_only/final/predictions.jsonl`\n- CodeLlama-13B exception_only: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/codellama13b_exception_only/final/predictions.jsonl`\n- DeepSeek exception_only (seed42): `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_exception_only/final/predictions.jsonl`\n\n如果某个路径不存在，用 `find /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/ -name \"predictions.jsonl\"` 定位。\n\n### 分析步骤\n1. 从每个 predictions.jsonl 提取错误样本（predicted != label）\n2. 比较错误样本集合：\n   - Qwen-1.5B vs Qwen-7B: overlap 几个？\n   - CodeLlama-13B vs Qwen-7B: overlap 几个？\n   - DeepSeek vs Qwen-7B: overlap 几个？\n   - 全模型共同错误（intersection）？\n3. 对共同错误样本：打印其 task_id/问题 ID 和真实标签，看是否有 ambiguity\n4. 计算 Cohen's Kappa 或 Jaccard index for error sets\n\n### 产出\n写一份简洁分析报告到 `/root/trace-verdict-study/analysis/prediction_overlap_report.md`，包含：\n- 错误样本 ID 列表对比\n- Overlap 统计\n- 共同错误样本的内容特征分析\n- 结论：是否支持 \"universal irreducible ceiling\" 假说\n\n**零 GPU 成本**，纯 CPU 分析。",
```
